    <script>
        // =====================================================================
        // STATE MANAGEMENT
        // =====================================================================
        let currentScreen = 0;
        const totalScreens = 7;
        let isCriticalAlert = false;
        let alertDurationSeconds = 0;
        let alertIntervalTimer = null;
        let hasAutoEscalated = false;

        // Normal Baseline Telemetry
        const normalVitals = {
            hr: 74,
            spo2: 98,
            temp: 36.8,
            motion: 0.97,
            air: 184
        };

        // Critical Telemetry Values (Respiratory Distress + Tachycardia Anomaly)
        const criticalVitals = {
            hr: 138,     // Severe Tachycardia (+64 BPM over baseline)
            spo2: 87,    // Severe Hypoxia (-11% drop)
            temp: 39.4,   // High Fever / Heat-Stress
            motion: 2.45, // Agitation / Spasmodic Tremor
            air: 462     // Hazardous Air / Gas Exposure
        };

        // =====================================================================
        // DOM ELEMENTS
        // =====================================================================
        const smartwatchFrame = document.getElementById('smartwatchFrame');
        const watchScreen = document.getElementById('watchScreen');
        const screens = document.querySelectorAll('.screen');
        const dots = document.querySelectorAll('.dot');
        const crownBtn = document.getElementById('crownBtn');
        const sideBtn = document.getElementById('sideBtn');
        const btnTestAlert = document.getElementById('btnTestAlert');
        const sosBtn = document.getElementById('sosBtn');
        const sosBtnText = document.getElementById('sosBtnText');
        const autoEscalationTag = document.getElementById('autoEscalationTag');

        // Home Elements
        const healthStatusHeader = document.getElementById('healthStatusHeader');
        const healthStatusText = document.getElementById('healthStatusText');
        const healthSubtext = document.getElementById('healthSubtext');
        const tickerText = document.getElementById('tickerText');
        const statusBrandNode = document.getElementById('statusBrandNode');
        
        // Vitals Cards Elements
        const homeCardHR = document.getElementById('homeCardHR');
        const homeCardSpO2 = document.getElementById('homeCardSpO2');
        const homeCardTemp = document.getElementById('homeCardTemp');
        const homeCardMotion = document.getElementById('homeCardMotion');

        const homeHR = document.getElementById('homeHR');
        const homeSpO2 = document.getElementById('homeSpO2');
        const homeTemp = document.getElementById('homeTemp');
        const homeMotion = document.getElementById('homeMotion');

        // Screen 1: Vitals Elements
        const vitalRowHR = document.getElementById('vitalRowHR');
        const vitalRowSpO2 = document.getElementById('vitalRowSpO2');
        const vitalRowTemp = document.getElementById('vitalRowTemp');
        const vitalRowAir = document.getElementById('vitalRowAir');
        const vitalsHR = document.getElementById('vitalsHR');
        const vitalsSpO2 = document.getElementById('vitalsSpO2');
        const vitalsTemp = document.getElementById('vitalsTemp');
        const vitalsAir = document.getElementById('vitalsAir');
        const vitalsTagHR = document.getElementById('vitalsTagHR');
        const vitalsTagSpO2 = document.getElementById('vitalsTagSpO2');
        const vitalsTagTemp = document.getElementById('vitalsTagTemp');
        const vitalsTagAir = document.getElementById('vitalsTagAir');

        // Screen 2: Baseline Elements
        const bCardHR = document.getElementById('bCardHR');
        const bCardSpO2 = document.getElementById('bCardSpO2');
        const bValHR = document.getElementById('bValHR');
        const bValSpO2 = document.getElementById('bValSpO2');
        const bValTemp = document.getElementById('bValTemp');
        const bDevHR = document.getElementById('bDevHR');
        const bDevSpO2 = document.getElementById('bDevSpO2');
        const bDevTemp = document.getElementById('bDevTemp');

        // Screen 3: Fall Elements
        const fallStatus = document.getElementById('fallStatus');
        const fallIcon = document.getElementById('fallIcon');
        const motionVal = document.getElementById('motionVal');
        const motionFill = document.getElementById('motionFill');
        const fallAlertText = document.getElementById('fallAlertText');

        // Screen 4: Air Quality Elements
        const airValue = document.getElementById('airValue');
        const airBadge = document.getElementById('airBadge');

        // Screen 5 & 6 Elements
        const tBuffer = document.getElementById('tBuffer');
        const tLatency = document.getElementById('tLatency');
        const sysBadge = document.getElementById('sysBadge');

        // =====================================================================
        // NAVIGATION SYSTEM
        // =====================================================================
        function goToScreen(index) {
            if (index < 0 || index >= totalScreens) return;
            
            screens.forEach((screen, i) => {
                screen.classList.toggle('active', i === index);
            });
            
            dots.forEach((dot, i) => {
                dot.classList.toggle('active', i === index);
            });

            currentScreen = index;
        }

        // Crown Button Navigation
        crownBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            goToScreen((currentScreen + 1) % totalScreens);
        });

        // Side Button Toggles Alert Mode
        sideBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            toggleCriticalAlert();
        });

        // Dot Navigation
        dots.forEach(dot => {
            dot.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                goToScreen(parseInt(e.target.dataset.index, 10));
            });
        });

        // Swipe & Mouse Drag Detection
        let touchStartX = 0;
        let touchEndX = 0;
        let isDragging = false;

        watchScreen.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
            touchStartX = e.changedTouches[0].screenX;
        }, { passive: true });

        watchScreen.addEventListener('touchend', e => {
            touchEndX = e.changedTouches[0].screenX;
            handleHorizontalSwipe();
        }, { passive: true });

        watchScreen.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
            isDragging = true;
            touchStartX = e.screenX;
        });

        watchScreen.addEventListener('mouseup', e => {
            if (!isDragging) return;
            isDragging = false;
            touchEndX = e.screenX;
            handleHorizontalSwipe();
        });

        function handleHorizontalSwipe() {
            const swipeDistance = touchStartX - touchEndX;
            if (Math.abs(swipeDistance) > 40) {
                if (swipeDistance > 0) {
                    goToScreen((currentScreen + 1) % totalScreens); // Swipe Left -> Next
                } else {
                    goToScreen((currentScreen - 1 + totalScreens) % totalScreens); // Swipe Right -> Prev
                }
            }
        }

        // Keyboard Shortcuts
        window.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
            if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
                goToScreen((currentScreen + 1) % totalScreens);
            } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
                goToScreen((currentScreen - 1 + totalScreens) % totalScreens);
            } else if (e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') {
                toggleCriticalAlert();
            }
        });

        // =====================================================================
        // ALARM & EMERGENCY ALERT SYSTEM (All User Requirements)
        // =====================================================================
        function toggleCriticalAlert() {
            isCriticalAlert = !isCriticalAlert;
            if (isCriticalAlert) {
                activateCriticalAlert();
            } else {
                deactivateCriticalAlert();
            }
        }

        btnTestAlert.addEventListener('click', toggleCriticalAlert);

        function activateCriticalAlert() {
            isCriticalAlert = true;
            alertDurationSeconds = 0;
            hasAutoEscalated = false;

            // 1. Visual Urgency: Haptic Shake & Screen Border Flash
            smartwatchFrame.classList.add('haptic-shake');
            watchScreen.classList.add('alert-border-flash');
            document.body.classList.add('alert-active');

            // 2. Visual Urgency: Flashing Ring & Non-Diagnostic Directives
            healthStatusHeader.innerText = "⚠️ CRITICAL HEALTH ALERT";
            healthStatusText.innerText = "RESPIRATORY DISTRESS";
            healthSubtext.innerText = "PHYSIOLOGICAL ANOMALY · ESCALATED";
            statusBrandNode.innerText = "● EMERGENCY ALARM";

            // 3. Visual Urgency: Color Shift (#4a0000 on Failing Metrics)
            homeCardHR.classList.add('alerting');
            homeCardSpO2.classList.add('alerting');
            homeCardTemp.classList.add('alerting');

            vitalRowHR.classList.add('alerting');
            vitalRowSpO2.classList.add('alerting');
            vitalRowTemp.classList.add('alerting');

            bCardHR.classList.add('alerting');
            bCardSpO2.classList.add('alerting');

            // 4. Content & Messaging: Vitals Screen Tags Out of Baseline
            vitalsTagHR.innerText = "CRITICAL";
            vitalsTagHR.className = "vital-tag";
            vitalsTagSpO2.innerText = "OUT OF BASELINE";
            vitalsTagSpO2.className = "vital-tag";
            vitalsTagTemp.innerText = "ELEVATED";
            vitalsTagTemp.className = "vital-tag";

            // 5. Behavioral Urgency: Dominant SOS Button
            sosBtnText.innerText = "⚠️ CRITICAL: SEND SOS NOW";

            // 6. Explainability Ticker Output
            tickerText.innerText = "⚠️ EDGE-AI ESCALATION: SpO₂ dropped to 87% (-11.2% deviation) · HR tachycardia (138 BPM, +64) · Extreme heat index · Caretaker notified";

            // Update System Screen Test Button
            btnTestAlert.innerText = "✓ RESET TO NORMAL STATE";
            btnTestAlert.classList.add('active-alert');
            sysBadge.innerText = "⚠ EDGE ESCALATION ACTIVE";
            sysBadge.style.color = "#ef4444";
            tBuffer.innerText = "3/3 Multi-Spikes (Escalated)";
            tBuffer.style.color = "#ef4444";

            // Apply Critical Telemetry Values Immediately
            applyTelemetryValues(criticalVitals);

            // 7. Auto-Escalation Timer (Forced Navigation to Screen 0 after 5s)
            clearInterval(alertIntervalTimer);
            alertIntervalTimer = setInterval(() => {
                alertDurationSeconds++;

                if (!hasAutoEscalated && alertDurationSeconds >= 5) {
                    hasAutoEscalated = true;
                    // Force navigation back to Screen 0 (Home Screen)
                    if (currentScreen !== 0) {
                        goToScreen(0);
                    }
                    autoEscalationTag.innerText = "⚠️ AUTO-ESCALATED: RETURNED TO EMERGENCY HOME SCREEN";
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        autoEscalationTag.innerText = "";
                    }, 4000);
                }
            }, 1000);
        }

        function deactivateCriticalAlert() {
            isCriticalAlert = false;
            alertDurationSeconds = 0;
            hasAutoEscalated = false;
            clearInterval(alertIntervalTimer);
            autoEscalationTag.innerText = "";

            // Remove Shake & Border Flash
            smartwatchFrame.classList.remove('haptic-shake');
            watchScreen.classList.remove('alert-border-flash');
            document.body.classList.remove('alert-active');

            // Reset Home Ring & Status
            healthStatusHeader.innerText = "OVERALL HEALTH STATUS";
            healthStatusText.innerText = "LOW";
            healthSubtext.innerText = "HEALTHY";
            statusBrandNode.innerText = "● VITALSYNC EDGE";

            // Reset Card Backgrounds
            homeCardHR.classList.remove('alerting');
            homeCardSpO2.classList.remove('alerting');
            homeCardTemp.classList.remove('alerting');

            vitalRowHR.classList.remove('alerting');
            vitalRowSpO2.classList.remove('alerting');
            vitalRowTemp.classList.remove('alerting');

            bCardHR.classList.remove('alerting');
            bCardSpO2.classList.remove('alerting');

            // Reset Tags
            vitalsTagHR.innerText = "Resting";
            vitalsTagHR.className = "vital-tag tag-green";
            vitalsTagSpO2.innerText = "Optimal";
            vitalsTagSpO2.className = "vital-tag tag-blue";
            vitalsTagTemp.innerText = "Normal";
            vitalsTagTemp.className = "vital-tag tag-green";

            // Reset SOS Button
            sosBtnText.innerText = "SEND EMERGENCY SOS";

            // Reset Ticker
            tickerText.innerText = "● ALL PHYSIOLOGICAL PARAMETERS WITHIN PERSONAL BASELINE NORMS";

            // Reset System Screen
            btnTestAlert.innerText = "🚨 TEST ALERT STATE";
            btnTestAlert.classList.remove('active-alert');
            sysBadge.innerText = "● ALL SYSTEMS NOMINAL";
            sysBadge.style.color = "#4ade80";
            tBuffer.innerText = "Clean (0/3 Spikes)";
            tBuffer.style.color = "#f8fafc";

            // Apply Normal Baseline Telemetry
            applyTelemetryValues(normalVitals);
        }

        function applyTelemetryValues(data) {
            // Home Screen
            homeHR.innerText = data.hr;
            homeSpO2.innerText = data.spo2;
            homeTemp.innerText = data.temp;
            homeMotion.innerText = data.motion;

            // Vitals Screen
            vitalsHR.innerText = data.hr;
            vitalsSpO2.innerText = data.spo2;
            vitalsTemp.innerText = data.temp;
            vitalsAir.innerText = data.air;

            // Baseline Screen Deviations
            const hrDiff = (data.hr - 72).toFixed(1);
            const spo2Diff = (data.spo2 - 98).toFixed(1);
            const tempDiff = (data.temp - 36.7).toFixed(1);

            bValHR.innerText = `${data.hr}.0 BPM`;
            bValSpO2.innerText = `${data.spo2}.0 %`;
            bValTemp.innerText = `${data.temp} °C`;

            bDevHR.innerText = `${hrDiff > 0 ? '+' : ''}${hrDiff} BPM (${hrDiff > 10 ? 'ANOMALY · Z: +3.2' : 'Nominal · Z: +0.4'})`;
            bDevSpO2.innerText = `${spo2Diff}% (${spo2Diff < -5 ? 'HYPOXIC DEVIATION · Z: -2.8' : 'Stable · Z: 0.0'})`;
            bDevTemp.innerText = `${tempDiff > 0 ? '+' : ''}${tempDiff} °C (${tempDiff > 1.5 ? 'HYPERTHERMIA · Z: +3.1' : 'Nominal · Z: +0.2'})`;

            // Motion & Fall Screen
            motionVal.innerText = `${data.motion} g`;
            motionFill.style.width = `${Math.min(data.motion * 35, 100)}%`;
            if (data.motion > 2.0) {
                fallStatus.innerText = "TREMOR / AGITATION";
                fallStatus.style.color = "#ef4444";
                fallIcon.innerText = "⚠️";
                fallAlertText.innerText = "● HIGH ACCELERATION / AGITATION EVENT";
                fallAlertText.style.color = "#ef4444";
                motionFill.style.background = "#ef4444";
            } else {
                fallStatus.innerText = "REST";
                fallStatus.style.color = "#22c55e";
                fallIcon.innerText = "🏃";
                fallAlertText.innerText = "● ACCELERATION STABLE — NO FALL";
                fallAlertText.style.color = "#4ade80";
                motionFill.style.background = "#22c55e";
            }

            // Air Screen
            airValue.innerText = data.air;
            if (data.air > 350) {
                airBadge.innerText = "HAZARDOUS EXPOSURE";
                airBadge.style.background = "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2)";
                airBadge.style.color = "#f87171";
            } else {
                airBadge.innerText = "SAFE ENVIRONMENT";
                airBadge.style.background = "rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.15)";
                airBadge.style.color = "#4ade80";
            }
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // REALISTIC TELEMETRY CLOCK & LIVE JITTER SIMULATION
        // =====================================================================
        function updateClock() {
            const now = new Date();
            const timeStr = now.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour12: false, hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
            document.getElementById('watchTime').innerText = timeStr;
        }
        updateClock();
        setInterval(updateClock, 1000);

        // Ambient Vitals Fluctuation (When NOT in Alert Mode)
        setInterval(() => {
            if (!isCriticalAlert) {
                const liveData = {
                    hr: 74 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) - 2,
                    spo2: Math.min(100, Math.max(96, 98 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) - 1)),
                    temp: (36.8 + (Math.random() * 0.4) - 0.2).toFixed(1),
                    motion: (0.97 + (Math.random() * 0.08) - 0.04).toFixed(2),
                    air: 184 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8) - 4
                };
                applyTelemetryValues(liveData);
                tLatency.innerText = `${12 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 6)} ms`;
            }
        }, 2000);

        // =====================================================================
        // EMERGENCY SOS BUTTON INTERACTION
        // =====================================================================
        sosBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const originalText = sosBtnText.innerText;
            sosBtnText.innerText = "DISPATCHING CARETAKER SOS...";
            sosBtn.style.background = "#7f1d1d";

            setTimeout(() => {
                sosBtnText.innerText = "✓ SOS SENT (GPS COORDINATES TRANSMITTED)";
                sosBtn.style.background = "#22c55e";

                setTimeout(() => {
                    sosBtnText.innerText = originalText;
                    sosBtn.style.background = "";
                }, 3000);
            }, 1200);
        });

        // Initialize with Normal Telemetry
        applyTelemetryValues(normalVitals);
    </script>
</body>
</html>
